<script>(function(){
  var root=document.getElementById('tt-art');if(!root||root.getAttribute('data-ready'))return;root.setAttribute('data-ready','1');
  var items=[].slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.tta-it')),N=items.length,cur=0,lastFocus=null;
  if(/#arttitles/.test(location.hash))root.classList.add('tta-edit');   /* add #arttitles to the URL to see which titles are still placeholders */
  var more=root.querySelector('.tta-mbtn');
  if(more)more.addEventListener('click',function(){root.classList.remove('tta-collapsed');var f=root.querySelector('.tta-more');if(f)f.focus();});
  /* lightbox lives on <body> so position:fixed is never trapped inside a transformed Squarespace wrapper */
  var lb=document.getElementById('tta-lb');
  if(!lb){lb=document.createElement('div');lb.id='tta-lb';lb.setAttribute('role','dialog');lb.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');lb.setAttribute('aria-label','Glitch art viewer');
    lb.innerHTML='<div class="tta-st"><img alt=""></div><div class="tta-ctr" aria-live="polite"></div>'+
      '<button type="button" class="tta-b tta-x" aria-label="Close viewer">✕</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="tta-b tta-p" aria-label="Previous image">‹</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="tta-b tta-n" aria-label="Next image">›</button>'+
      '<div class="tta-cap"><p class="tta-ct"></p><p class="tta-cm"></p></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(lb);}
  var img=lb.querySelector('img'),ct=lb.querySelector('.tta-ct'),cm=lb.querySelector('.tta-cm'),ctr=lb.querySelector('.tta-ctr'),st=lb.querySelector('.tta-st');
  var rm=window.matchMedia&&matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  function full(a,w){var h=a.getAttribute('href');if(a.hasAttribute('data-alt'))return h;if(/pbs\.twimg\.com/.test(h))return h.replace(/([?&]name=)\w+/,'$1'+(w>=2500?'orig':'large'));return h.replace(/\?format=\d+w$/,'')+'?format='+w+'w';}
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
  function preload(i){var a=items[(i+N)%N];var p=new Image();p.src=full(a,1500);}
  function show(i,dir){
    cur=(i+N)%N;var a=items[cur],t=a.querySelector('.tta-t').textContent;
    img.src=full(a,1500);img.srcset=full(a,1000)+' 1000w, '+full(a,1500)+' 1500w, '+full(a,2500)+' 2500w';img.sizes='100vw';
    img.alt=t;img.width=+a.getAttribute('data-w');img.height=+a.getAttribute('data-h');
    ct.textContent=t;ctr.textContent=(cur+1)+' / '+N;
    var post=a.getAttribute('data-post'),d=a.getAttribute('data-date');
    var src=a.getAttribute('data-src'),meta=[src?'via '+esc(src):'',d?esc(d):''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    cm.innerHTML=(meta?'<span>'+meta+'</span>':'')+'<a class="tta-xb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"'+(/^https:\/\/(x|twitter)\.com\//.test(post||'')?' href="'+esc(post)+'"':' hidden')+'>View post on X ↗</a>';
    if(dir&&!rm){lb.classList.remove('tta-gl');void lb.offsetWidth;lb.classList.add('tta-gl');}
    preload(cur+1);preload(cur-1);
  }
  function open(i){lastFocus=document.activeElement;show(i,0);lb.classList.add('tta-on');document.documentElement.classList.add('tta-lock');
    lb.querySelector('.tta-x').focus();document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey,true);}
  function close(){lb.classList.remove('tta-on','tta-gl');document.documentElement.classList.remove('tta-lock');document.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey,true);
    img.removeAttribute('src');img.removeAttribute('srcset');if(lastFocus&&lastFocus.focus)lastFocus.focus();}
  function onKey(e){var k=e.key;
    if(k==='Escape'||k==='Esc'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();close();}
    else if(k==='ArrowRight'){e.preventDefault();show(cur+1,1);}
    else if(k==='ArrowLeft'){e.preventDefault();show(cur-1,-1);}
    else if(k==='Tab'){var f=[].slice.call(lb.querySelectorAll('button,a[href]')),i=f.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      if(e.shiftKey&&i<=0){e.preventDefault();f[f.length-1].focus();}else if(!e.shiftKey&&i===f.length-1){e.preventDefault();f[0].focus();}}}
  [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.tta-grid'),function(g){var grp=[].slice.call(g.querySelectorAll('.tta-it'));grp.forEach(function(a,i){a.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.button||e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey||e.shiftKey)return;e.preventDefault();items=grp;N=grp.length;open(i);});});});
  lb.querySelector('.tta-x').addEventListener('click',close);
  lb.querySelector('.tta-p').addEventListener('click',function(){show(cur-1,-1);});
  lb.querySelector('.tta-n').addEventListener('click',function(){show(cur+1,1);});
  st.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===st)close();});   /* tap the dark backdrop to close */
  /* swipe: left/right = prev/next, down = close */
  var sx=0,sy=0,tr=false;
  lb.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){if(e.touches.length!==1){tr=false;return;}tr=true;sx=e.touches[0].clientX;sy=e.touches[0].clientY;},{passive:true});
  lb.addEventListener('touchend',function(e){if(!tr)return;tr=false;var t=e.changedTouches[0],dx=t.clientX-sx,dy=t.clientY-sy;
    if(Math.abs(dx)>45&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.2)show(cur+(dx<0?1:-1),dx<0?1:-1);
    else if(dy>90&&Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx)*1.5)close();},{passive:true});
})();</script>

<script>(function(){var g=document.getElementById('tt-gap');if(!g||g.getAttribute('data-gx'))return;g.setAttribute('data-gx','1');function v(){g.classList.toggle('gx-paused',document.hidden);}document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',v);v();})();</script>
<script>(function(){var W=window,K='__ttGlitchTitle',D=document;if(W[K]&&W[K].stop)W[K].stop();var h=D.querySelector('#tt-gap .gap-h');if(!h)return;var T='GLITCH ART',G='!<>/\\[]{}=+*#%?01ABCDEFGHKMNRSTXZ',L=[h.querySelector('.s1'),h.querySelector('.s2'),h.querySelector('.s3')],tm=0,iv=0,dead=0,api;function set(t){h.setAttribute('data-text',t);for(var i=0;i<L.length;i++)if(L[i])L[i].textContent=t;}function rm(){return !!(W.matchMedia&&W.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);}function halt(){clearTimeout(tm);clearInterval(iv);tm=iv=0;set(T);}function stop(){dead=1;halt();D.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',vis);if(W[K]===api)W[K]=null;}function ok(){if(dead)return false;if(!h.isConnected){stop();return false;}return true;}function sched(){clearTimeout(tm);tm=setTimeout(burst,5500+Math.random()*5000);}function burst(){tm=0;if(!ok())return;if(D.hidden||rm())return sched();var f=0,n=12;clearInterval(iv);iv=setInterval(function(){if(!ok())return;f++;var k=Math.floor(f/n*T.length),o='';for(var i=0;i<T.length;i++)o+=(i<k||T[i]===' ')?T[i]:G.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random()*G.length));if(f>=n){clearInterval(iv);iv=0;set(T);sched();}else set(o);},55);}function vis(){if(!ok())return;if(D.hidden)halt();else if(!tm&&!iv)sched();}api={stop:stop,el:h};W[K]=api;D.addEventListener('visibilitychange',vis);set(T);sched();})();</script>
